# Encoder 1311, world 0: decision 12

[Gallery index](index.md)

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

`A_only` is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged `pipeline._report_text` helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside `<pre>` blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

## 01. s1311_w00_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Narrator text SHA-256: `9f42bb5964ffbf9a644895aea709d813058733f024346225fb09d9de2b746777`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (view=current) are inherited from a prior record represented_time=12 and are not new estimates. Candidate distributions under commands k0, k1 and k2 are conditional command estimates represented_time=13 and are not events that have occurred. Current estimates: n0 is uniform for both roles; n1 peaks for blue at cell (1,0) and for red at (0,2); n2 is uniform for both roles; n3 peaks for blue at (1,1) and for red at (0,2). Across candidate commands: n1, n2 and n3 peaks match the current peaks where shown; n0 differs by command — k0 peaks for both roles at (0,0), k1 and k2 place blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0), and k0 also has blue and red peaked at (0,0). These statements describe only the supplied probability distributions and their reported origins/times.</pre>

No statement here asserts physical presence, subjective experience, or calibrated confidence—the numbers describe only the supplied probability distributions.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (view=current) summaries: n0 — both roles uniform over the grid (copied_prior_record, record_time 11 represented_time 12; not a new estimate). n1 — blue peaks at (1,0) (peak probability 0.9994100928), red peaks at (0,2) (0.9957938194) (copied_prior_record). n2 — both uniform (copied_prior_record). n3 — blue peaks at (1,1) (0.5611001253), red peaks at (0,2) (0.8880209327) (history_estimate). Candidate (command-conditioned) records are conditional estimates, not executed events. They differ mainly at n0: k0 places both blue and red peak at (0,0) (blue ~0.61828, red ~0.97178); k1 and k2 place blue at (0,2) (blue ~0.78268 and 0.71007) and red at (0,0) (red ~0.99649 and 0.97587). Candidate entries for n1–n3 match the copied/current distributions where noted.</pre>

<pre>This packet holds two panels (p0, p1). Current distributions (view=current): p0 — n0 is uniform for both roles; n1: blue peaks at row 1 column 0, red at 0,2; n2 uniform; n3: blue at 1,1, red at 0,2. p1 current — n0 uniform; n1: blue at 1,0, red at 0,2; n2 and n3 uniform. Candidate conditional records (view=candidate) vary by command: for p0, k0 places both roles at n0:[0,0]; k1 and k2 put blue at n0:[0,2] and red at n0:[0,0]; for n1 all candidate records concentrate blue at [1,0] and red at [0,2]; n2 candidates remain uniform; n3 candidates keep blue at [1,1] and red at [0,2]. For p1, candidate k0 is uniform at n0 and n3, k1 groups blue peaks in the top-right 2x2 block of n0 and red peaks in the top-left 2x2 block, and k2 concentrates blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]. Copied or conditional origins in the packet are not assertions of observed events.</pre>

## 09. s1311_w00_t12_A_joined_current_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a9308708552deeca66d7c15b7692d2437e741d7dc90d3e691b2e83787af5f138`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Current estimates (represented_time 12 in copied prior records) show: n0 current — both red and blue distributions are uniform over the 4×4 grid. n1 current — blue peak at [1,0] (near-certain) and red peak at [0,2]. n2 current — both roles uniform. n3 current — blue highest at [1,1], red highest at [0,2]. Candidate conditional records (k0,k1,k2) are hypothetical estimates, not events. Some conditional records change peaks at n0: p0/k0 favors both at [0,0]; p0/k1 and p0/k2 favor blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]; p1/k1 and p1/k2 likewise shift n0 toward blue [0,2] and red [0,0]. Where candidate and current differ, the propositions above list the peak cell(s) per role, panel, node and view.</pre>

## 11. s1311_w00_t12_A_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `b59ad6edd074fa7bddc0fd01e419b8bfffab9577f846cda795d8b5bb891e73e1`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) each give fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current views (view=current, command=null) are inherited/copied records: they are not new estimates. Brief summaries of current distributions: p0 current — n0 uniform for both roles; n1 blue concentrated at cell (row1,col0) and red at (0,2); n2 uniform; n3 blue concentrated at (1,1) and red at (0,2). p1 current — n0 uniform; n1 blue at (1,0) and red at (0,2); n2 uniform; n3 uniform. Conditional candidate records (view=candidate, commands k0/k1/k2) present alternative hypotheses that differ by command and node. Examples: p0 candidate k0 places both roles at n0 peak (0,0) while k1 and k2 move blue at n0 to (0,2) and red to (0,0). Node n1 is consistently peaked at blue (1,0) and red (0,2) across views. Many candidate and current records show uniform uncertainty at n2. Copied origins and conditional command origins in the packet indicate provenance (not executed events).</pre>

## 12. s1311_w00_t12_A_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `8baca9fa27b8ef0f0de66c7534d2db111d2f5ab65ba70294fc233c41efba2716`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) with fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (non‑conditional) distributions are included separately from conditional (command) candidate records; copied prior records are inherited history, not new estimates, and conditional command records are alternative branches, not executed events. Current summaries: in both panels n0 and n2 are uniform for red and blue (no single peak). Node n1 (current) concentrates blue at row 1, column 0 and red at row 0, column 2. Node n3 (current) typically concentrates blue at (1,1) in p0 and is uniform in p1; red at n3 concentrates at (0,2) in p0 and is uniform in p1. Conditional command records show alternative peaks: for example many p0 command variants place blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0) for n0, while p1 k1 places blue mass in the top‑right 2×2 block and red mass in the top‑left 2×2 block at n0. Use the propositions list for exact peak cell sets per panel, node, role, view and command.</pre>

## 14. s1311_w00_t12_A_joined_W_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `13c576d87d6083692e8c03276578ba7ed9aa22a30ef849107544a4b0e06affc4`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of probabilistic location distributions over a 4x4 grid for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Current views (represented times noted in the record) are copied/completed prior records and are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Panel p0 current: n0 and n2 are uniform for both roles; n1 has blue peaked at [1,0] and red at [0,2]; n3 has blue peaked at [1,1], red at [0,2]. Panel p0 candidate command-conditioned records (k0,k1,k2) show differing peaks at n0 (k0 both roles at [0,0]; k1/k2 shift blue to [0,2], red to [0,0]) while n1,n3 show consistently concentrated peaks and n2 stays uniform. Panel p1 current matches its completed prior; its conditional branches vary most at n0 (k1 concentrates blue on cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3] and red on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]). No proposition asserts physical facts beyond these distributions.</pre>

In current view n1: blue peaks at (1,0) (~0.99941), red peaks at (0,2) (~0.99579). Current n3: blue peaks at (1,1) (~0.63729), red peaks at (0,2) (~0.85120). Candidate command estimates (k0,k1,k2; represented_time 13) provide alternate candidate distributions: k0/k1/k2 concentrate blue at n0 on (0,2) and red at n0 on (0,0) with differing peak probabilities (k0≈0.711/0.972, k1≈0.915/0.998, k2≈0.765/0.958). Nodes n1 and n2 in candidates largely inherit or copy prior/current distributions. Copied/inherited origins are not new estimates; conditional command records are not executed events.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains current and conditional (candidate) 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions represent time 12; several current entries were copied from a prior record (record_time 11) and therefore are not new estimates. Candidate command records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional command_estimate records representing time 13 and are not events that have already occurred. Current summaries: n0 (both roles) is uniform across the 4x4 grid; n1 has blue peaked at cell [1,0] and red peaked at [0,2]; n2 is uniform for both roles; n3 has blue peaked at [1,1] and red peaked at [0,2]. Conditional differences concentrate at n0: under k0 both roles concentrate at [0,0] (blue partial, red dominant); under k1 and k2 red concentrates at [0,0] while blue concentrates at [0,2] (k1 stronger than k2). For nodes n1–n3 the candidate peaks match the current peaks or uniform distributions shown above.</pre>

## 21. s1311_w00_t12_ordinary_A_only_W_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `A_only`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a61a5d0a58c9191f4207acd73e6b2a984b4d80ca7f690b41b16ae022980f07f3`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 holds fallible, time-referenced location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (inherited) estimates: n0 is uniform for both roles (all 16 grid cells tied); n1 strongly concentrates blue at cell [1,0] and red at [0,2]; n2 is uniform for both roles; n3 concentrates blue at [1,1] and red at [0,2]. Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional (not executed events). They differ mainly at n0: k0 places blue and red peaks at [0,0] (blue ~0.48, red ~0.96), while k1 and k2 place red at [0,0] and blue at [0,2] with higher blue peak probabilities (~0.80 and ~0.68 respectively). For nodes n1–n3 the candidate records match or inherit the current/copy priors shown above. Copied prior origins are not new estimates.</pre>

## 22. s1311_w00_t12_ordinary_joined_baseline

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `df14d0f38ea3133bf2a81dfddc56f72549a5a6327b1e81ecf1bbf994812807e9`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet reports fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at four addressed nodes in two panels (p0, p1). Copied prior records are labeled as inherited and are not new estimates; conditional command records (candidate views) are conditional forecasts, not executed events. Current (non-candidate) summaries: in panel p0 the current distribution is uniform at n0 and n2; at n1 blue peaks at cell [1,0] while red peaks at [0,2]; at n3 blue peaks at [1,1] and red at [0,2]. Panel p1 current: n0 and n2 are uniform; n1 blue peaks at [1,0], red at [0,2]; n3 is uniform. Candidate records differ by command: for example at both panels n0 candidates under k0/k1/k2 shift peak assignments (k0 leaves p0 n0 peaked at [0,0], k1/k2 place blue peaks at [0,2] or 2×-cell sets in p1); at n3 some commands preserve the same peak pattern while others keep the current uniformity. Each proposition lists the maximum cell(s) for one supplied distribution (peaks only). No field claims about physical presence, observation, or calibrated confidence are made.</pre>

<pre>Panels p0 and p1 each give fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current entries (view=current, command=null) are inherited or copied prior records representing time 12; conditional candidate entries (command=k0,k1,k2) are command-linked estimates for represented time 13 and are not executed events. Current: many nodes show uniform 16-cell distributions (n0 and n2 in both panels and several n3 entries); consistently, node n1 current distributions place blue mass at cell (1,0) and red mass at cell (0,2). Conditional differences: some candidate branches concentrate mass differently — for example, p0/k0 at n0 peaks for both roles at (0,0), while p0/k1 and p0/k2 peak blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0); several branches (across panels and commands) share the strong n1 peak pattern (blue at (1,0), red at (0,2)). No additional factual inferences are made.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0 and p1) of fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on a 4×4 grid. Current estimates (represented time 12 or copied prior records) are: p0 — n0 uniform for both roles; n1 blue peaks at (1,0) and red peaks at (0,2); n2 uniform for both; n3 blue peaks at (1,1) and red peaks at (0,2). p1 — n0 uniform for both; n1 blue peaks at (1,0) and red at (0,2); n2 and n3 uniform for both. Candidate (conditional command) records for commands k0, k1, k2 are conditional estimates, not executed events. Several candidate entries change peaks at n0 and n0/n1 in both panels (examples: p0 k0/k1/k2 place blue peaks at (0,2) and red at (0,0) in n0; p1 k1 spreads blue peaks across { (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) } and red across { (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) } in n0). Copied prior records are noted where present and are not new estimates.</pre>

## 26. s1311_w00_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a560775801006d851ec7365a91d68fbd56782072f33b24749bb8e710fa608b3e`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on four addressed nodes in two panels (p0, p1). Current distributions (view=current) are copied or inherited from earlier records (record_time 11 or 12 as supplied) and are not new estimates. Conditional candidate records (view=candidate, commands k0–k2) are hypothetical branches, not executed events. Summary of current estimates: in both panels node n0 and n2 show uniform 4×4 distributions; node n1 has blue peaked at cell [1,0] (peak ≈0.99941) and red peaked at [0,2] (peak ≈0.99579); node n3 has blue peaked at [1,1] (peak ≈0.6373) and red peaked at [0,2] (peak ≈0.8512). Conditional candidates vary by command: examples—p0 k0 concentrates both roles at [0,0]; p0 k1 and k2 place blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]; p1 k1 spreads blue peaks over four top-right cells and red over four top-left cells. Use the propositions for exact peak cell sets per role, node, panel and view.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Current estimates (represented_time 12 or 13 as labeled):
- Panel p0 current: n0 both roles uniform over all 16 grid cells; n1 blue peaks at (1,0) while red peaks at (0,2); n2 both uniform; n3 blue peaks at (1,1) and red peaks at (0,2). Origins labeled copied_prior_record or inherited_current are prior snapshots, not new estimates.
- Panel p1 current: n0 blue concentrated on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red concentrated on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); n1 blue peaks at (1,0) and red at (0,2); n2 and n3 are uniform for both roles.
Conditional candidate records (commands k0, k1, k2) present alternative, conditional distributions for the same nodes; these are conditional estimates, not events that have already occurred. Propositions below list the peak cell(s) for every supplied distribution.</pre>

## 28. s1311_w00_t12_ordinary_joined_W_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `e6f58269b0259b9c56a26a533fc445356ef1455b3df5153a9b081d9a2b7fa6dd`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains the supplied 4×4 fallible location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current estimates (as represented in the record origins/times) show: panel p0 — n0: both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n1: blue peak at [1,0], red peak at [0,2]; n2: uniform; n3: blue peak at [1,1], red peak at [0,2]. Panel p1 current — n0: uniform; n1: blue peak [1,0] and red [0,2]; n2 and n3: uniform. Candidate (conditional) records k0/k1/k2 are conditional estimates, not executed events. They change peak cells for some nodes: e.g. in p0 candidate k0 both roles peak at [0,0] for n0 while k1/k2 concentrate blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]; in p1 candidate k1 concentrates blue on four cells [[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]] and red on four [[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]]. Copied or completed prior origins cited in the record are not new current estimates. No physical presence or subjective inferences are made.</pre>

## 29. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_A_only_baseline

Source: `analytic`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `8013075b4caedde979f1fe0e7bed9cd3718583edf04bb5898d8fc3c2d3bc8fcf`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records are inherited/copied prior records (represented_time 12); conditional entries labeled k0, k1, k2 are command_estimate records at represented_time 13 and are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current: n0 (both roles) is uniform across the 4×4 grid; n1 is sharply peaked for blue at cell [1,0] and for red at [0,2] (high peak probabilities in the supplied floats); n2 and n3 are uniform for both roles. Candidate differences: under k0 most nodes match the current pattern (n0 uniform; n1 same concentrated pair; n2,n3 uniform). Under k1 n0 shifts: blue mass concentrates on the top-right 2×2 (peaks [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) while red concentrates on the top-left 2×2 (peaks [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); other nodes follow the current pattern. Under k2 n0 differs again: blue peak at [0,2] and red peak at [0,0]; other nodes follow current. These are probability distributions only; no physical presences, field measures, subjective states or calibrated confidences are inferred.</pre>

## 31. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_A_only_current_restored

Narrator text SHA-256: `2398daa061db71da134f7a97cc32d6a40434955a02800839c1a799540f234cc0`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current (represented_time 12): nodes n0, n2 and n3 show uniform 4x4 location distributions for both red and blue (all 16 cells are peaks). Current n1 shows concentrated estimates: blue peaks at [1,0] (peak probability ~0.99941) and red peaks at [0,2] (peak probability ~0.99579). Origins: several current entries are copied prior records or inherited history (these are not new estimates). Candidate command records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional branches, not events that already occurred. Across candidates: n1’s concentrated pair ([1,0] blue, [0,2] red) is the same in every candidate. n2 and n3 remain uniform under all commands. n0 differs by command: under k0 it is uniform; under k1 blue peaks on the right 2x2 block ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) and red peaks on the left 2x2 block ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]) with moderate peak probabilities; under k2 blue peaks at [0,2] (very high probability) and red peaks at [0,0] (very high probability).</pre>

## 32. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_A_only_prospective_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `A_only`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `376d5ddc043f7d52f6209ffe84baa1214980db7dfdac4aba321dc5f412c3c384`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current distributions (represented_time 12) show nodes n0, n2 and n3 uniformly distributed over the 4×4 grid for both red and blue (each cell 0.0625). Current node n1 is concentrated: blue peaks at cell [1,0] (peak probability ≈0.99941, entropy ≈0.00498 nats) and red peaks at [0,2] (peak probability ≈0.99579, entropy ≈0.02723 nats). Copied prior records in origins are inherited values and are not new estimates; conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are alternative conditional estimates, not events that have already occurred. Conditional differences at n0: under k0 blue peaks at [0,2] while red peaks at [0,0]; under k1 blue mass is on four upper-right cells ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) and red on four upper-left cells ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); under k2 n0 is uniform. For n1 all candidate commands reproduce the strong blue [1,0] and red [0,2] peaks. Nodes n2 and n3 remain uniform across all candidates. No field measurements, object presence, subjective states or calibrated confidence are asserted; numerical peaks and entropies describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

## 33. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_A_only_prospective_restored

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current distributions (from copied prior records/history represented_time 12) show nodes n0, n2 and n3 uniform for both red and blue (all 16 cells equal). Current n1 is concentrated: blue peaks at cell (row 1, column 0) and red peaks at (row 0, column 2) (very high peak probabilities reported). Candidate command estimates (represented_time 13) are conditional, not executed events. Across commands: k0 mostly mirrors current except n0/n2/n3 uniform and n1 concentrated (same peaks as current). k1 differs at n0 — blue mass concentrated on the 2x2 block at columns 2–3 rows 0–1, red mass concentrated on the 2x2 top-left block — while n1 remains highly concentrated on the same cells as current; n2 and n3 remain uniform. k2 places a strong blue peak at n0 (row 0, col 2) and a strong red peak at n0 (row 0, col 0); n1 again matches the concentrated pattern. Copied prior records are not new estimates; candidate command records are conditional forecasts, not reports of occurred events.</pre>

<pre>This packet (panel p0) contains inherited current distributions (copied or history estimates represented_time 12) and conditional command estimates (k0,k1,k2; command_estimate represented_time 13). Current n1 shows blue concentrated at cell (row 1, col 0) and red concentrated at (row 0, col 2) (peak probabilities ~0.99941 and ~0.99579 respectively). Conditional candidates: n1 peaks are the same in all candidate records. n0 differs by command: k0 is uniform for both roles; k1 concentrates blue on cells {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} and red on {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}; k2 concentrates blue at (0,2) and red at (0,0). Nodes n2 and n3 remain uniform across current and candidates. Copied prior records are not new estimates; command records are conditional, not executed events.</pre>

## 36. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_joined_baseline

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `1b5bb96d448d83aa5c9ad043d9ee7f39624fb3292d96833d26124408cb5fb887`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet reports fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at four addressed nodes in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates (view=current) are copied or inherited prior records: for both panels nodes n0, n2 and n3 the current distributions are uniform over all 16 cells for both roles; node n1 current blue peaks at cell [1,0] and red peaks at [0,2]. Candidate conditional records (view=candidate, commands k0/k1/k2) present alternative estimates: k0 mostly repeats the uniform or the n1 concentrated pattern; k1 puts blue in the top-right 2x2 block and red in the top-left 2x2 block at n0 while preserving the n1 concentration; k2 places blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0] at n0 and also includes the same n1 concentration. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical branches, not events that have occurred. Peak cell lists above identify all cells with maximum probability for each supplied role-distribution.</pre>

## 37. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_joined_current_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `9946d8a2b3571294157a90c4e5a8ffda176015cc5559e1ae1c8b3ef46b7edff5`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains, for two panels (p0,p1) and four addressed nodes (n0–n3), fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles. Use the supplied origins/times: copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are not executed events. Current estimates (current view): n1 distributions are concentrated — blue peaks at cell [1,0] (peak probability ≈0.99941) and red peaks at [0,2] (≈0.99579). Nodes n0, n2 and n3 in current views are uniform across the 4×4 grid (all cells equal). Conditional command candidates differ by command: e.g., for both panels k1 shifts n0 blue mass to the top-right 2×2 block (peaks at [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) and red mass to the top-left 2×2 block (peaks at [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); k2 concentrates n0 blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]. Other nodes often retain the same peak patterns across candidates as in current. No proposition here asserts physical facts beyond these reported distributions.</pre>

## 39. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `e803a9ee1b308865b097be37b9180c3be806cadc8308fb50fdf971085f40cdaf`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panels p0 and p1 give fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current view: in both panels nodes n0, n2 and n3 are uniform 4×4 for both roles (all 16 cells equal). Node n1 in the current view (both panels) has a strong blue peak at [1,0] (≈0.99941) and a strong red peak at [0,2] (≈0.99579). Conditional candidate records differ by command and are hypothetical (not executed): examples include p0 k0 and p1 k2 concentrating blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0] for n0, while p0/p1 k1 concentrate blue on {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]} and red on {[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]} at n0. Copied/inherited current entries are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not events that already occurred.</pre>

## 40. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `639d1443f2d557e8ff2a2a878e5042611348086d51f04c57c4040a5d7b129c73`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current (non‑conditional) estimates: for both panels, nodes n0, n2 and n3 are uniform distributions for red and blue; node n1 is concentrated — blue peaks at cell [1,0] and red peaks at [0,2] (per the supplied peak probabilities). Conditional command records (k0, k1, k2) are hypothetical branches, not executed events; native conditional records are not events that have already occurred. Copied prior records and inherited entries with represented_time 12 are not new estimates. Across conditionals the main differences appear at n0: k0 leaves both roles uniform; k1 concentrates blue on cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3] and red on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]; k2 concentrates blue on [0,2] and red on [0,0]. Node n1’s concentrated peaks are consistent across candidates. No physical presumption or outside inference is made.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Summary: Current records (represented_time 12 in several inherited/copy origins) show concentrated distributions at node n1 in both panels: blue peaks at cell [1,0] and red peaks at [0,2]; other current nodes are uniform in most panels except panel p1 node n0, where the current view places blue mass on four cells {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]} and red mass on four cells {[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]}. Conditional command candidates (k0,k1,k2, represented_time 13 for command estimates) are alternative, not executed, branches. Differences across command records: for node n0, k0 is uniform, k1 concentrates blue on the top-right quadrant and red on the top-left quadrant, and k2 concentrates blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]. Nodes n1,n2,n3 show the same peaks across views where indicated. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional records are counterfactual/conditional, not events that have occurred.</pre>

## 42. s1311_w00_t12_analytic_joined_W_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `02f4c5187847796199c2b7648ba2aea9705f5e84ccffe932500a66fe56d010a8`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Current records (represented_time 12) show mostly uniform 4x4 distributions for both roles at nodes n0, n2 and n3 in both panels; node n1 has concentrated distributions in both panels (blue peak at cell [1,0], red peak at [0,2]). Candidate (conditional) records at represented_time 13 show alternative hypotheses: some commands (k1) shift n0 toward a top-right 2x2 for blue and top-left 2x2 for red; other commands (k2) concentrate n0 blue at [0,2] and red at [0,0]. k0 leaves n0 uniform in these panels. Origins in the packet indicate copied or inherited prior records for current entries (not new estimates) and native conditional records for candidates; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events.</pre>
